- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Enrico Dandolo, leader of Venice (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Venice
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Merchant of Venice, Replacing Great Merchant (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Great Galleass, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Building: Piazza San Marco, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Civilization Ability: Cannot create Settlers or annex Cities. Trade Route cap doubled, target restrictions removed. Free Merchant of Venice at Trade. Puppets have -30% Yield penalties, can use Gold and gain Happiness like normal Cities.
      - Leader: Enrico Dandolo
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Peace, Land, Bread (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Peace, Land, Bread: -2 Unhappiness from Poverty and Growth increased by 20% in all Cities. Building maintenance reduced by 20%.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 8
        - Boldness: 1
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarmongerHate: 9
        - NeutralBias: 5
        - FriendlyBias: 6
        - GuardedBias: 6
        - AfraidBias: 10
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Friendliness: 7
        - WorkWithWillingness: 8
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 3
        - Loyalty: 10
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 1
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 4
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 1
        - Forgiveness: 8
        - Meanness: 1
        - Neediness: 5
        - Chattiness: 5
        - DeceptiveBias: 1
        - Rationale: Maintaining cautious defensive persona. Current values remain optimal: AfraidBias at 10 recognizes Portugal's superior military (taking 34+62+93 damage in one turn on our Gatling Gun 13057). Boldness/WarBias at 1 prevents suicidal offensive actions. Loyalty and WorkWithWillingness at maximized values ensure unwavering support for Sweden - our survival depends entirely on their military protection. Portugal's continuing aggression with Machine Guns advancing into Venice territory requires maximum defensive caution. Nuclear deterrence is still absent (0 Uranium), making diplomacy and Swedish alliance our primary survival mechanism.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Electronics
        - Rationale: Continuing Electronics research. With Venice's newly discovered Uranium resource, long-term nuclear deterrence is now possible. Electronics (Police Station for city security) completes in ~10 turns, then pursue Nuclear Fission to unlock nuclear weapons. Portugal has 9 Uranium and Nuclear Fission likely researched, pressing urgency for Venice's own nuclear capability. Electronics provides immediate security benefits while positioning for nuclear deterrence against Portugal's ongoing aggression.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Peace, Land, Bread (Policy)
        - Rationale: Adopting Peace, Land, Bread (Order Level 2 Tenet) remains optimal choice. Provides -2 Unhappiness from Poverty, +20% Growth, and -20% Building Maintenance. Venice currently has 4 GPT income but previous deficits; reduced building maintenance helps stabilize economy. Growth bonus supports long-term economic base for defense and eventual nuclear weapons production. Does not delay military production, prioritizing economic stability while Sweden provides military protection.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Copenhagen
        - Public: -20
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: Copenhagen (a Copenhagen city-state allied with Egypt) has declared war on Venice. This is an unacceptable act of aggression that must be met with proportional diplomatic response. Copenhagen is a key Egyptian ally controlling the Islam-majority city-state, making this an Egyptian proxy attack. Setting negative relationship to reflect hostile posture while maintaining principled opposition to aggression that targets peaceful civilizations.
        - UpdatedTurn: 229
        
        ### France
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: France declared war on Venice despite previous friendship relationship (50/50), killing Venice's Cruiser and Great Galleass. This betrayal completely upends Venice's World Congress strategy as France was the main voting ally. Setting negative relationship to reflect hostile posture. France's attack also caused three city-states (Ife, Kyzyl, Okilis) to declare war on Venice. Venice must defend against this aggression while exploring whether the war can be ended through diplomacy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### Siam
        - Public: -5
        - Private: -5
        - Rationale: Slightly reducing hostility toward Siam from -15 to -10. Siam's Cold War proposal benefits Freedom ideology players (Portugal, Egypt) but harms Order nations like Venice and Sweden. However, with World Congress voting in 2 turns and Venice dependent on Sweden, maintaining minimal diplomatic pressure without further antagonizing Egypt's vassal Siam is prudent. The slight easing signals willingness to negotiate while recognizing Siam's role in Egypt's coalition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 378
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: -40
        - Private: -40
        - Rationale: Increasing hostility toward Portugal from -35 to -40. Turn 398 events show escalating Portuguese aggression: new Submarine ID 15007 created in Lisbon, Machine Gun ID 14410 operating at Deserts Hills 12,12 attacking Venice's Holy Site, Machine Gun ID 14154 at Plains Land 11,13 taking 21 damage from Venetian counterattack. Portuguese Submarine traded damage with Swedish Submarine protecting Venice (Submarine 13751 went from 54% to 28% HP absorbing attacks). Portugal's continuing offensive operations despite Swedish defense demonstrates existential threat - maximum principled opposition required.
        - UpdatedTurn: 399
        
        ### Egypt
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Keeping Egypt relationship at neutral. Turn 398 shows Egypt focusing on internal development with Archaeologist 15037 created in Memphis and multiple caravans being established. Egypt's Tank 14676, Landships 11957/12033, Artillery 12552 are positioned far from Venice territory at Desert Land 44,37/Plains Hills 54,42. While Egypt's World Religion (Islam) and 15 delegate votes influence World Congress, Egypt is not threatening Venice militarily. Maintaining neutral relationship avoids unnecessary diplomatic conflict while Venice focuses on defense against Portugal.
        - UpdatedTurn: 399
        
        ### Sweden
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Maintaining neutral relationship with Sweden (our overlord). The previous neutral setting (0/0) from Turn 399 remains appropriate. Swedish forces are actively defending Venice against Portugal - Fleet Destroyers, Infantry, and Anti-Aircraft Guns are holding the line alongside our units. Sweden is Venice's survival path as a vassal, and our diplomatic votes in the upcoming World Congress (6 turns) will support Sweden's proposals. No change needed to our cooperative diplomatic stance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 418
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Maintaining current defensive strategy remains optimal. Critical development: Venice just discovered and improved Uranium at 13,13 (events 78-79), enabling future nuclear deterrence. Sweden's coalition (Jet Fighters, Heavy Bombers, Mobile SAMs, Fleet Destroyers) continues providing robust defense against Portuguese aggression. Venice's Arsenal completes in 3 turns, further strengthening city defense. Portuguese Machine Guns and Infantry remain active near our territory, requiring maximum defensive caution. World Congress voting in 8 turns - Venice must support Sweden's proposals. Nuclear weapons not yet viable (Manhattan Project complete but need time for Uranium extraction and Nuclear Fission tech), so diplomacy remains primary survival mechanism. No strategic changes needed.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
        
        ### Flavors

        - Defense: 100
        - Mobilization: 20
        - CityDefense: 95
        - MilitaryTraining: 70
        - Recon: 40
        - Ranged: 85

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - French Sphere of Influence: Kyzyl:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Sanction: Sweden:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Siamese Sphere of Influence: Jerusalem:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - World Ideology: Freedom:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 413
          - Siamese Sphere of Influence: Belgrade:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Siamese Sphere of Influence: Oviedo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Egyptian Sphere of Influence: Copenhagen:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Egyptian Sphere of Influence: Hanoi:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Spaceflight Regulations:
            - Description: Spaceflight Regulations control the proliferation of modern ballistic technology, oversee national space programs, and limit the advance of pontentially-dangerous technology.
              As long as this resolution is active, all players will suffer a global -25% Production penalty for Spaceship Parts and +25% Gold purchase costs for Spaceship Parts.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: 424
          - World Religion: Islam:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: 405
          - Swedish Sphere of Influence: Ecbatana:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 405
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: Egypt
          - Enact: Open Door: Ife:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - Proposer: Portugal
          - Enact: Siamese Sphere of Influence: Dorestad:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - Proposer: Siam

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Enrico Dandolo, leader of Venice (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Venice
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Merchant of Venice, Replacing Great Merchant (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Unit: Great Galleass, Replacing Galleass (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Building: Piazza San Marco, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Civilization Ability: Cannot create Settlers or annex Cities. Trade Route cap doubled, target restrictions removed. Free Merchant of Venice at Trade. Puppets have -30% Yield penalties, can use Gold and gain Happiness like normal Cities.
      - Leader: Enrico Dandolo
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Rocketry: Allows you to build the Guided Missile, a one-time use unit specialized in damaging City garrisons.
        Completing it would unlock: Nuclear Fission, Radar
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Peace, Land, Bread (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Peace, Land, Bread: -2 Unhappiness from Poverty and Growth increased by 20% in all Cities. Building maintenance reduced by 20%.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Workers' Faculties (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Worker Faculties: Factories increase City Science output by 10%. +100% Production towards Factories, and receive 5 Free Factories in your Empire.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Equality (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness in every City and +1 Happiness for every 15 Citizens in a City.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from and Distress, Poverty, Illiteracy, and Boredom in all Cities.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Fraternity (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +3 Food in every City.
        - * +3 Science from City Connections.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Rocketry is the direct prerequisite to Nuclear Fission, which unlocks nuclear weapons. Given Portugal's massive 9 Uranium stockpile and Freedom ideology, Venice must secure nuclear deterrence as soon as possible. Since we have successfully constructed a Quarry on our Uranium source at 13,13, researching Rocketry next and then Nuclear Fission is our fastest path to establishing defensive nuclear capabilities.
        - Technology: Rocketry
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Adopting Peace, Land, Bread is crucial for our economy. It reduces building maintenance by 20%, which is vital as our 93 GPT trade deal with Sweden is expiring in 2 turns and we risk a major deficit. It also provides a 20% growth boost and reduces unhappiness from poverty, making it the most impactful Tier 1 Order tenet for our single-city empire.
        - Policy: Peace, Land, Bread
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
        - Flavors:
          - Naval: 98
          - UseNuke: 80
          - Ranged: 85
          - Diplomacy: 90
          - CityDefense: 95
          - Nuke: 80
          - Espionage: 85
          - Antiair: 95
          - Gold: 100
          - Growth: 65
          - Defense: 100
          - Production: 95
          - MilitaryTraining: 70
          - Air: 90
          - Science: 90
      - rationale: Maintaining our defensive and economic posture under the UnitedNations grand strategy. Extremely high Defense (100) and CityDefense (95) ensure Venice remains secure alongside Sweden's protecting forces. Gold (100) and Diplomacy (90) are prioritized to help Venice fund and utilize its 26 trade route slots and leverage city-state alliances. High Production (95) and Science (90) support our push toward defensive military and nuclear technologies.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 39157
    - reasoningTokens: 7870
    - outputTokens: 371
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Completing Construction**
            
            The Uranium quarry construction has just wrapped up! This marks a significant step in our resource development. I'm now evaluating how best to leverage this new asset, considering its immediate implications for our strategic position as a vassal of Sweden.
            
            **Investigating Resource Flow**
            
            My focus has shifted to understanding the Uranium discrepancy. The quarry is built, yet the resource count is zero. I'm investigating if this is a turn delay, a consequence of our vassal status with Sweden, or another factor influencing immediate resource availability.
            
            **Confirming Research Path**
            
            I've clarified the research progression; Computers are in progress, and I've confirmed Electronics is an available next step. I am now selecting Electronics as our subsequent technological pursuit.
            
            **Selecting Policy Options**
            
            With research set, I'm now reviewing policy choices. I am considering the available Tier 1 and Tier 2 Order policies, evaluating their strategic impact.
            
            **Choosing Policy Direction**
            
            I've reviewed the available Order policies, noting options like People's Army and Five-Year Plan. Considering our recent strategic shifts and potential diplomatic entanglements, particularly with Egypt due to Sweden's alliance, I'm re-evaluating our immediate policy direction.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Status**
            
            I'm re-examining Portugal's stance. They've denounced Venice and are at war with France, yet hold a peace treaty with Sweden. The presence of their Anti-Aircraft Gun in our territory, despite open borders, needs further clarification regarding its strategic implications.
            
            **Clarifying Troop Movements**
            
            I'm analyzing the unit positions and alliance statuses. Portugal has units near our territory and is allied with Sweden, yet France, our enemy, is at war with Portugal. This complex web necessitates a careful assessment of potential threats and diplomatic maneuvers, especially given our ongoing conflict with Egypt and its allies.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Alliances**
            
            I'm reassessing our alliances and enemies. France has allied M'banza-Kongo, and we're at war with Copenhagen and Hanoi. Portugal's complex relationship with Sweden, France, and their units on our land requires careful consideration, especially with our ongoing conflict against Egypt.
            
            **Assessing War Effort**
            
            I'm analyzing Sweden's war score against Egypt, noting they appear to be holding their own despite Egypt's significant power and tourism. I'm now investigating how we might influence this, perhaps by boosting Sweden's culture or reducing Egypt's tourism, especially considering the active Cold War resolution impacting trade and alliances.
            
            **Selecting Research Direction**
            
            My current focus is on steering our research. Despite the AI's apparent inclination towards "Computers," I've identified our actual available options and am now prioritizing "Electronics" to unlock crucial infrastructure like Police Stations and future advancements in energy and telecommunications.
            
            **Re-evaluating French Relations**
            
            I'm clarifying France's current stance towards us and Sweden. Despite recent peace, their denouncements suggest lingering animosity that demands careful observation and potential adjustments to our diplomatic strategy given their proximity and past aggression.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Entanglements**
            
            I'm piecing together the recent diplomatic shifts, particularly how our peace with France was automatically established through Sweden. I'm now scrutinizing Portugal's proximity and their units' presence, considering the complex alliances and active conflicts on the map, especially with France and Egypt.
            
            **Analyzing Swedish Defense**
            
            I've observed Sweden's substantial military concentration near Venice, indicating a robust defensive posture in our vicinity. My current focus is on understanding the zero Uranium count despite the completed quarry, investigating potential ties to our vassal status or turn-based resource delays.
            
            **Investigating Uranium Discrepancy**
            
            I'm confirming if Sweden takes a percentage of my Uranium production or if the quarry's output hasn't registered yet. Regardless, securing nuclear technology is critical given other powers' stockpiles, and I need to re-verify diplomatic standings.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Nuances**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our relationship with Portugal. While our current -40/-40 stance promotes caution and defense, I'm questioning if a hostile relationship value automatically provokes conflict, especially as our war with Egypt is inexplicably marked at 0/0. This warrants further investigation into AI diplomatic responses.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Modifiers**
            
            I'm re-evaluating how diplomatic modifiers impact city-states versus major civilizations. It seems my focus on relationship values for France and Siam is correct, but Copenhagen, being a city-state, might operate differently, suggesting I need to adjust my approach to city-state diplomacy.
            
            **Focusing on Major Civs**
            
            I've realized I cannot directly influence city-states like Copenhagen. My efforts should solely target major civilizations, specifically Sweden, Portugal, France, Egypt, and Siam, to adjust relationships and advance our strategic goals.
            
            **Evaluating Grand Strategy**
            
            My current Grand Strategy is `UnitedNations`, but I am questioning if this remains the most optimal path. I am re-examining victory conditions, particularly our own limited diplomatic vote count and Sweden's position, to determine the best strategic direction moving forward.
            
            **Revising Grand Strategy**
            
            I'm reconsidering our primary objective. Given our limited capacity for independent victory as a vassal, maximizing our score and survival is key. I'm now evaluating the `UnitedNations` Grand Strategy, focusing on its strengths in Gold, Science, Food, and City-State alliances, which align well with Venice's unique abilities.
            
            **Analyzing Trade Routes**
            
            I'm reviewing our trade route capacity and current usage. It appears we have many available slots but are not maximizing their potential, which is impacting our low Gold Per Turn. I am also analyzing current deals with Sweden, noting the significant incoming GPT from coal that will expire soon, highlighting an impending financial deficit.
            
            **Exploring Trade Route Utilization**
            
            I'm investigating why our numerous trade route slots remain unused. My current hypothesis involves checking for plundering due to the Egypt conflict and will shift focus to building Caravans/Cargo Ships post-Arsenal completion to boost gold.
            
            **Selecting Research Path**
            
            I've corrected my understanding of the tech tree. Rocketry, not Electronics, unlocks Nuclear Fission. Therefore, I will pivot our research to prioritize Rocketry to achieve our nuclear ambitions sooner.
            
            **Prioritizing Rocketry**
            
            I've identified a crucial correction in my research path. Rocketry directly unlocks Nuclear Fission, superseding the previous consideration of Electronics. My immediate focus is now prioritizing Rocketry to expedite our nuclear deterrence capabilities.
            
            **Selecting Order Policies**
            
            I am now focusing on Order policies. I’ve noted the economic benefits of “Peace, Land, Bread” and its impact on unhappiness and building maintenance, which directly addresses my concerns about Gold per Turn. I'm still assessing if other Tier 1 options are as strategically impactful for my singular city.
            
            **Adopting Policy Tenet**
            
            I've confirmed "Peace, Land, Bread" as the optimal Tier 1 Order policy for my single city due to its growth and building maintenance benefits, solidifying my choice. Relationships with other major powers remain stable and do not require immediate adjustment.
            
            **Setting AI Flavors**
            
            I'm reinforcing our AI's core priorities by explicitly setting its flavors for Gold, Diplomacy, and Defense using `set-flavors`, ensuring alignment with our current Grand Strategy of `UnitedNations`. This proactive step prevents any drift in the AI's decision-making.
            
            **Refining Defense Strategy**

            - Rationale: Adopting Peace, Land, Bread is crucial for our economy. It also provides a 20% growth boost and reduces unhappiness from poverty, making it the most impactful Tier 1 Order tenet for our single-city empire.
            - Policy: Peace, Land, Bread

          - providerOptions: undefined

High Production (95) and Science (90) support our push toward defensive military and nuclear technologies.
